# IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD characters (P3): leaders are characters recruited in history (common/characters/COLDWAR_GER_DDR_1970_leaders.txt), switched with promote_character.
# IMPERIA COLD WAR — German leadership and Eastern portrait integration.
COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_choose_1_rainer_barzel_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				original_tag = GER
				exists = yes
				is_dynamic_country = no
				IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
				is_ai = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GER_1970_dark_regime }
			}
			western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Rainer Barzel" ruling_only = yes } }
		}
		set_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_leader = { name = "Rainer Barzel" ruling_only = yes } }
			}
			# IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD characters (P3): character from history
			if = { limit = { has_character = COLDWAR_GER_1970_leader_rainer_barzel } promote_character = COLDWAR_GER_1970_leader_rainer_barzel }
		}
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_observe = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_EU_1970_note_GER_rainer_barzel_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = GER
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		set_variable = { cwger_cdu_leader = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_EU_1970_GER_observe = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_choose_0_alexander_schalck_golodkowski_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				original_tag = DDR
				exists = yes
				is_dynamic_country = no
				IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
				is_ai = no
			}
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = { OR = {
				has_country_leader = { name = "Alexander Schalck-Golodkowski" ruling_only = yes }
				has_country_leader = { name = "COLDWAR_DDR_leader_alexander_schalck_golodkowski" ruling_only = yes }
			} }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_preference_0 = 1 }
		COLDWAR_DDR_clear_heirs = yes
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_ulb_retiring
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_cc_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { OR = {
					has_country_leader = { name = "Alexander Schalck-Golodkowski" ruling_only = yes }
					has_country_leader = { name = "COLDWAR_DDR_leader_alexander_schalck_golodkowski" ruling_only = yes }
				} }
			}
			# IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD characters (P3): character from history
			if = { limit = { has_character = COLDWAR_DDR_1970_leader_alexander_schalck_golodkowski } promote_character = COLDWAR_DDR_1970_leader_alexander_schalck_golodkowski }
		}
		set_correct_ideology_trait = yes
		COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_observe = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_EU_1970_DDR_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			original_tag = DDR
			exists = yes
			is_dynamic_country = no
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			is_ai = no
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_ulb
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_hon
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_DDR_first_sec_sto

			}
